# cubemain: PD2 vs vanilla 1.13c

Generated by `tools/data/build_tables.py`. Vanilla = `patch_d2.mpq` (stock 1.13c); PD2 = `pd2data.mpq`.
Key column: `description` (duplicates suffixed `#n` by occurrence). Cells compared after trimming spaces; empty and `0` count as equal (the game reads an empty numeric cell as 0).

| | vanilla | PD2 |
|---|---:|---:|
| rows | 151 | 2341 |
| columns | 105 | 105 |

Summary: 2193 rows added, 3 removed, 143 changed, 148 kept rows at a different row index. 0 columns added, 0 removed, 0 renamed.

D2 links most tables by row index, so inserted/reordered rows shift every later index.
